1\end{bmatrix},

(262h)

where (a) is obtained by knowing that an
s\in[0,1]
exists, such that this expression holds by the MVT.

Setting the desired upper bound from (
260
) gives

{\frac{d}{dt}V^{\delta}({{\hat{\bm{x}}},{\bm{z}}})}\leq\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\delta}}\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&0\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\delta}}\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}\\
1\end{bmatrix}\leq 0.

(263)

It suffices to enforce this inequality for
{\bm{\eta}}\in H
since
H
is a convex polytopic set.

Applying the S-procedure to enforce that the inequality holds
\forall{\bm{\delta}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}{\bm{\delta}}\geq\delta^{2}
with

\displaystyle{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\delta}}\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}\\
1\end{bmatrix},

(264a)

\displaystyle F_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}-{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix},

(264b)

\displaystyle F_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&0\end{bmatrix},

(264c)

results in the following LMI:

\displaystyle{\lambda^{\delta}}\begin{bmatrix}-{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&0\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\succeq 0

(265a)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&0\end{bmatrix}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\begin{bmatrix}-{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0

(265b)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0,

(265c)

with
{\lambda^{\delta}}\geq 0
.

We want this LMI to hold
\forall{\bm{\epsilon}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}{\bm{\epsilon}}\leq\epsilon^{2}
. Applying the S-procedure again, this time with

\displaystyle{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}{\bm{\delta}}\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}\\
1\end{bmatrix},

(266a)

\displaystyle F_{1}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&-\epsilon^{2}\end{bmatrix},

(266b)

\displaystyle F_{2}

\displaystyle\coloneqq\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix},

(266c)

results in the following LMI:

\displaystyle{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\begin{bmatrix}0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&-\epsilon^{2}\end{bmatrix}-\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\succeq 0

(267a)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\begin{bmatrix}0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&-\epsilon^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0

(267b)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0,

(267c)

with
{\lambda^{\delta}},{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\geq 0
. Pre- and post-multiplying by
\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&1\end{bmatrix}
yields

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&1\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{P^{\delta}}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC&{P^{\delta}}LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0

(268a)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}{P^{\delta}}+{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}+{\lambda^{\delta}}&LC&LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
{P^{\delta}}^{-1}(LC)^{\top}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&\bm{0}\\
0^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}&{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\bm{0}&\bm{0}&1\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0

(268b)

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}^{\top}+{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&LC{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
{P^{\delta}}^{-1}(LC)^{\top}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\preceq 0.

(268c)

Filling in the definition of
{A^{\mathrm{cl}}}
gives

\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}\left({A({\bm{\zeta}})}+{B({\bm{\zeta}})}{K^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\top}+\left({A({\bm{\zeta}})}+{B({\bm{\zeta}})}{K^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}\right){P^{\delta}}^{-1}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&LC{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
{P^{\delta}}^{-1}(LC)^{\top}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}\preceq 0,

(269)

which, under the coordinate change
{X^{\delta}}\coloneqq{P^{\delta}}^{-1},{Y^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}\coloneqq{K^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}{X^{\delta}}
yields the following LMI:

\begin{bmatrix}{A({\bm{\zeta}})}{X^{\delta}}+{B({\bm{\zeta}})}{Y^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}+\left({A({\bm{\zeta}})}{X^{\delta}}+{B({\bm{\zeta}})}{Y^{\delta}({\bm{x}})}\right)^{\top}+{\lambda^{\delta}}{X^{\delta}}&LC{X^{\delta}}&LF{\bm{\eta}}\\
\left(LC{X^{\delta}}\right)^{\top}&-{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}{X^{\delta}}&\bm{0}\\
\left(LF{\bm{\eta}}\right)^{\top}&\bm{0}&{\lambda^{\delta,\epsilon}}\epsilon^{2}-{\lambda^{\delta}}\delta^{2}\end{bmatrix}\preceq 0.

(270)

∎

Appendix I
Proof of norm LMI (
196d
)

The goal of this appendix is to show that (
197
) is enforced by LMI (
196d
).

Squaring (
197
) yields

\left\lVert{P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert^{2}\leq l^{2}.

(271)

Working out this inequality in matrix form gives

\displaystyle\left\lVert{P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right\rVert^{2}

\displaystyle=\left({P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)^{\top}\left({P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(272a)

\displaystyle\overset{\text{(a)}}{=}{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}{P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

(272b)

\displaystyle={P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}LC{P^{\delta}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}

(272c)

\displaystyle\leq l^{2}{I^{{n^{\mathrm{x}}}}},

(272d)

where (a) holds by symmetry of
{P^{\delta}}
. Multiplying both sides with
{P^{\delta}}^{\frac{1}{2}}
from left and right yields

(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}LC\preceq l^{2}{P^{\delta}},

(273)

or, equivalently,

l^{2}{P^{\delta}}-(LC)^{\top}{P^{\delta}}{P^{\delta}}^{-1}{P^{\delta}}LC\succeq 0.

(274)

Taking the Schur complement of this LMI by leveraging
{P^{\delta}}\succeq 0
gives

\begin{bmatrix}{P^{\delta}}&{P^{\delta}}LC\\
\left({P^{\delta}}LC\right)^{\top}&l^{2}{P^{\delta}}\end{bmatrix}\succeq 0,

(275)

which is equivalent to LMI (
196d
).
∎
